- **advisor-schwartz**: AI advisor based on Breakthrough Advertising by Eugene Schwartz (1966) — the foundational direct-response text on channeling mass desire into copy that converts. Diagnoses the market on two axes (5 states of awareness, 5 stages of sophistication), then channels the desire that already exists onto one offer via intensification, identification, gradualization, mechanism and redefinition. Every recommendation carries a citation tag [BTA:NN] and rewrites the user's actual copy (before/after), not just theory. Invoke explicitly via /advisor-schwartz. - **adyen-invalid-hmac-signature-on-webhoo-c7f8dec754d80048**: Fixes Adyen webhook handlers that reject notifications with Invalid HMAC signature. Not for notifications that arrive with no signature at all. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/adyen-invalid-hmac-signature-on-webhoo-c7f8dec754d80048))

- **adyen-invalid-paymentdata-checkout-14-89791ee0eafe2516**: Fixes Adyen Checkout error 14_003 Invalid paymentData on /payments/details calls.
